Let me write op_sequence_vec carefully:
```
def op_sequence_vec(h, n, deadline, steps, maxsub):
    try:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        L = n - 1
        M = build_M(h)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
        best = float(c.max())
        for d in steps:
            if d <= 0:
                continue
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            subs = 0
            improved_any = True
            while improved_any and subs < maxsub and time.time() < deadline:
                improved_any = False
                subs += 1
                for i in range(n):
                    if time.time() > deadline:
                        return project_h(h, n), compute_c5(h)
                    jv = np.arange(i + 1, n)
                    if jv.size < 1:
                        continue
                    hj = h[jv]
                    can_p = (h[i] <= 1.0 - d)
                    can_n = (h[i] >= d)
                    if not (can_p or can_n):
                        continue
                    D = M[i][None, :] - M[jv]
                    base_p = c[None, :] + d * D
                    base_p[:, L] -= 2.0 * d * d
                    nmP = base_p.max(axis=1)
                    base_n = c[None, :] - d * D
                    base_n[:, L] -= 2.0 * d * d
                    nmN = base_n.max(axis=1)
                    nm = np.full(jv.size, np.inf)
                    if can_p:
                        nm = np.where(hj >= d, nmP, nm)
                    if can_n:
                        nm = np.where(hj <= 1.0 - d, np.minimum(nm, nmN), nm)
                    order = np.argsort(nm)
                    chosen = -1; idx = -1; dirsign = 1
                    for kk in order:
                        val = nm[int(kk)]
                        if val < best - 1e-14:
                            idx = int(kk); chosen = int(jv[idx])
                            p_ok = can_p and (hj[idx] >= d)
                            n_ok = can_n and (hj[idx] <= 1.0 - d)
                            if p_ok and (not n_ok or nmP[idx] <= nmN[idx]):
                                dirsign = 1
                            else:
                                dirsign = -1
                            break
                    if chosen < 0:
                        continue
                    ii = i; jj = chosen
                    di = d if dirsign > 0 else -d
                    dj = -d if dirsign > 0 else d
                    dc = di * M[ii] + dj * M[jj]
                    M_update(M, ii, di, jj, dj, n)
                    c = c + dc
                    c[L] -= di * di + dj * dj
                    h[ii] += di
                    h[jj] += dj
                    best = float(c.max())
                    improved_any = True
                # after for i
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)
    except Exception:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)
```
Good.

Now, one concern: M[jv] where jv is an array — fancy indexing returns (J, C) copy. M[i][None,:] is (1,C). D = (1,C)-(J,C) → (J,C). base_p = c[None,:](1,C) + d*D(J,C) → (J,C). base_p[:,L] -= ... works (J entries modified at column L). Good.

Now, building M once and updating: after many accepts, M stays exact; c exact. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c5(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / len(h)))


def project_h(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(2000):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h * (1.0 + diff / tot), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if diff > 1e-13:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            ad = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            if ad <= 0:
                break
            h[idx] += ad
        elif diff < -1e-13:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sd = min(-diff, h[idx])
            if sd <= 0:
                break
            h[idx] -= sd
        else:
            break
    return h


def interp_resample(h0, n0, n1):
    if n0 == n1:
        return np.asarray(h0, dtype=float).copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
    x1 = (np.arange(n1) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n1)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h0)


def build_M(h):
    n = h.shape[0]
    L = n - 1
    t = np.arange(2 * n - 1) - L
    irows = np.arange(n)[:, None]
    im = irows - t[None, :]
    ip = irows + t[None, :]
    M = np.zeros((n, 2 * n - 1))
    imI = im.astype(int)
    ipI = ip.astype(int)
    m1 = (im >= 0) & (im < n)
    M[m1] += (1.0 - h[imI[m1]])
    m2 = (ip >= 0) & (ip < n)
    M[m2] -= h[ipI[m2]]
    return M


def M_update(M, i, di, j, dj, n):
    L = n - 1
    rows = np.arange(n)
    a = L + (rows - i)
    m = (a >= 0) & (a < 2 * n - 1)
    M[rows[m], a[m]] -= di
    b = L + (i - rows)
    m = (b >= 0) & (b < 2 * n - 1)
    M[rows[m], b[m]] -= di
    c1 = L + (rows - j)
    m = (c1 >= 0) & (c1 < 2 * n - 1)
    M[rows[m], c1[m]] -= dj
    d1 = L + (j - rows)
    m = (d1 >= 0) & (d1 < 2 * n - 1)
    M[rows[m], d1[m]] -= dj
    return M


def smax_obj(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    dx = 2.0 / n
    f = c * dx
    cm = float(f.max())
    keep = f > (cm - 40.0 / alpha)
    if not keep.any():
        return cm
    e = np.exp(alpha * (f[keep] - cm))
    return cm + float(np.log(e.sum()) / alpha)


def smax_grad(x, n, alpha):
    M = build_M(x)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    dx = 2.0 / n
    f = c * dx
    cm = float(f.max())
    P = np.zeros(2 * n - 1)
    keep = f > (cm - 40.0 / alpha)
    if keep.any():
        e = np.exp(alpha * (f[keep] - cm))
        P[keep] = e / e.sum()
    else:
        P[int(np.argmax(f))] = 1.0
    g = dx * (M @ P)
    return g


def csum_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)


def slsqp_stage(h, n, alpha, iters):
    try:
        cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': csum_fun, 'args': (n,)}]
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        res = minimize(smax_obj, h, args=(n, alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=smax_grad,
                       bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': iters, 'ftol': 1e-12})
        hx = project_h(res.x, n)
        return hx, compute_c5(hx)
    except Exception:
        return h, compute_c5(h)


def do_slsqp(h, n, dead, alphas=None):
    if alphas is None:
        alphas = [500, 1500, 6000, 20000]
    h = project_h(h, n)
    cur = h
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    for a in alphas:
        if time.time() > dead:
            break
        if n <= 260:
            iters = 40
        elif n <= 340:
            iters = 26
        else:
            iters = 16
        h1, c1 = slsqp_stage(cur, n, a, iters)
        cur = h1
        if c1 < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = project_h(h1, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def op_sequence_vec(h, n, deadline, steps, maxsub):
    try:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        L = n - 1
        M = build_M(h)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
        best = float(c.max())
        for d in steps:
            if d <= 0:
                continue
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            subs = 0
            improved_any = True
            while improved_any and subs < maxsub and time.time() < deadline:
                improved_any = False
                subs += 1
                for i in range(n):
                    if time.time() > deadline:
                        return project_h(h, n), compute_c5(h)
                    jv = np.arange(i + 1, n)
                    if jv.size < 1:
                        continue
                    hj = h[jv]
                    can_p = (h[i] <= 1.0 - d)
                    can_n = (h[i] >= d)
                    if not (can_p or can_n):
                        continue
                    D = M[i][None, :] - M[jv]
                    base_p = c[None, :] + d * D
                    base_p[:, L] -= 2.0 * d * d
                    nmP = base_p.max(axis=1)
                    base_n = c[None, :] - d * D
                    base_n[:, L] -= 2.0 * d * d
                    nmN = base_n.max(axis=1)
                    nm = np.full(jv.size, np.inf)
                    if can_p:
                        nm = np.where(hj >= d, nmP, nm)
                    if can_n:
                        nm = np.where(hj <= 1.0 - d, np.minimum(nm, nmN), nm)
                    order = np.argsort(nm)
                    chosen = -1
                    dirsign = 1
                    idx = -1
                    for kk in order:
                        val = nm[int(kk)]
                        if val < best - 1e-14:
                            idx = int(kk)
                            chosen = int(jv[idx])
                            p_ok = can_p and (hj[idx] >= d)
                            n_ok = can_n and (hj[idx] <= 1.0 - d)
                            if p_ok and (not n_ok or nmP[idx] <= nmN[idx]):
                                dirsign = 1
                            else:
                                dirsign = -1
                            break
                    if chosen < 0:
                        continue
                    ii = i
                    jj = chosen
                    if dirsign > 0:
                        di = d
                        dj = -d
                    else:
                        di = -d
                        dj = d
                    dc = di * M[ii] + dj * M[jj]
                    M_update(M, ii, di, jj, dj, n)
                    c = c + dc
                    c[L] -= di * di + dj * dj
                    h[ii] += di
                    h[jj] += dj
                    best = float(c.max())
                    improved_any = True
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)
    except Exception:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)


def pair_newmax(c, D, t, L):
    e = c + t * D
    e[L] = e[L] - 2.0 * t * t
    return float(e.max())


def golden_m(h, n, deadline, rounds):
    try:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        M = build_M(h)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
        L = n - 1
        best = float(c.max())
        ips = (5.0 ** 0.5 - 1.0) / 2.0
        for _ in range(rounds):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            improved = False
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                for jj in range(i + 1, n):
                    if time.time() > deadline:
                        break
                    lo = max(-h[i], h[jj] - 1.0)
                    hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[jj])
                    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
                        continue
                    D = M[i] - M[jj]
                    a2 = lo + ips * (hi - lo)
                    b2 = hi - ips * (hi - lo)
                    fa = pair_newmax(c, D, a2, L)
                    fb = pair_newmax(c, D, b2, L)
                    for _g in range(12):
                        if fa < fb:
                            hi = b2
                            b2 = a2
                            fb = fa
                            a2 = lo + ips * (hi - lo)
                            fa = pair_newmax(c, D, a2, L)
                        else:
                            lo = a2
                            a2 = b2
                            fa = fb
                            b2 = hi - ips * (hi - lo)
                            fb = pair_newmax(c, D, b2, L)
                    t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                    ft = pair_newmax(c, D, t, L)
                    if ft < best - 1e-13:
                        di = t
                        dj = -t
                        dc = di * M[i] + dj * M[jj]
                        M_update(M, i, di, jj, dj, n)
                        c = c + dc
                        c[L] -= di * di + dj * dj
                        h[i] += di
                        h[jj] += dj
                        best = float(c.max())
                        improved = True
            if not improved:
                break
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)
    except Exception:
        h = project_h(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)


def steps_for(n):
    if n <= 260:
        return [0.4, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002]
    return [0.3, 0.15, 0.07, 0.03, 0.015, 0.007, 0.0035, 0.0018, 0.001, 0.0006]


def maxsub_for(n):
    if n <= 240:
        return 26
    if n <= 320:
        return 16
    return 9


def optimize_node(h0, n, nd, use_slsqp):
    h = project_h(h0, n)
    cs0 = compute_c5(h)
    if use_slsqp:
        sl_dead = time.time() + 0.32 * max(1.0, nd - time.time())
        sl_dead = min(sl_dead, nd - 3)
        if sl_dead - time.time() > 3:
            hs, cs = do_slsqp(h, n, sl_dead)
            if cs < cs0 - 1e-15:
                h = hs
                cs0 = cs
    steps = steps_for(n)
    maxsub = maxsub_for(n)
    op_dead = time.time() + 0.62 * max(1.0, nd - time.time())
    op_dead = min(op_dead, nd - 2)
    h, cs = op_sequence_vec(h, n, op_dead, steps, maxsub)
    if n <= 300 and time.time() < nd - 4:
        if n <= 240:
            gr = 3
        else:
            gr = 2
        gl_dead = min(nd - 1, time.time() + 0.4 * max(1.0, nd - time.time()))
        h, cs = golden_m(h, n, gl_dead, gr)
    h = project_h(h, n)
    cs = compute_c5(h)
    return h, cs


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        run_dead = t0 + float(budget_s) - 12.0
    except Exception:
        run_dead = t0 + 108.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        pah = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        pn = len(pah)
        if pn < 8:
            pah = np.full(200, 0.5)
            pn = 200
    except Exception:
        pah = np.full(200, 0.5)
        pn = 200
    pah = project_h(pah, pn)
    bh = pah.copy()
    bc = compute_c5(pah)
    bn = pn

    nodes = [(200, 110), (240, 100), (280, 110), (320, 120), (360, 135), (400, 150)]
    for (n, cap) in nodes:
        if time.time() > run_dead - 60:
            break
        if bn != n:
            h_in = interp_resample(bh, bn, n)
        else:
            h_in = bh.copy()
        nd = min(time.time() + cap, run_dead - 60)
        if nd - time.time() < 12:
            continue
        try:
            topt = optimize_node(h_in, n, nd, True)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if topt[1] < bc - 1e-15:
            bc = topt[1]
            bh = topt[0]
            bn = n

    for n in [440, 470]:
        if time.time() > run_dead - 70:
            break
        if bn != n:
            h_in = interp_resample(bh, bn, n)
        else:
            h_in = bh.copy()
        nd = min(time.time() + 110, run_dead - 70)
        if nd - time.time() < 15:
            continue
        try:
            topt = optimize_node(h_in, n, nd, True)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if topt[1] < bc - 1e-15:
            bc = topt[1]
            bh = topt[0]
            bn = n

    for s in [0.02, 0.04, 0.08, 0.12]:
        if time.time() > run_dead - 45:
            break
        try:
            hp = bh + rng.normal(0.0, s, bn)
            hp = project_h(hp, bn)
            nd = min(time.time() + 55, run_dead - 45)
            if nd - time.time() < 12:
                continue
            topt = optimize_node(hp, bn, nd, False)
            if topt[1] < bc - 1e-15:
                bc = topt[1]
                bh = topt[0]
        except Exception:
            continue

    if time.time() < run_dead - 12:
        try:
            sl_dead = min(time.time() + 15, run_dead - 12)
            if sl_dead - time.time() > 4:
                fs, fc = do_slsqp(bh, bn, sl_dead, [20000, 40000, 80000, 150000])
                if fc < bc - 1e-15:
                    bc = fc
                    bh = fs
            fh, fc = op_sequence_vec(bh, bn, min(time.time() + 25, run_dead - 8),
                                     [0.001, 0.0005, 0.00025, 0.0001], 6)
            if fc < bc - 1e-15:
                bc = fc
                bh = fh
        except Exception:
            pass

    bh = project_h(bh, bn)
    bc = compute_c5(bh)
    return bh, bc, bn
```
